  async function loadJson(path) {

  // src/core/geo.js
  var EARTH_RADIUS_KM = 6371.0088;
  function toRad(deg) {
    return deg * Math.PI / 180;
  }
  function distanceKm(a, b) {
    const dLat = toRad(b.lat - a.lat);
    const dLon = toRad(b.lon - a.lon);
    const lat1 = toRad(a.lat);
    const lat2 = toRad(b.lat);
    const h = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(lat1) * Math.cos(lat2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;
    return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS_KM * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(h));
  }
  function distanceBetweenAirports(airportA, airportB) {
    return distanceKm(
      { lat: airportA.lat, lon: airportA.lon },
      { lat: airportB.lat, lon: airportB.lon }
    );

  function isAircraftAvailable(aircraft, year) {

  }
  function canBuyNewAircraft(aircraft, year) {

    if (aircraft.conversionOnly === true) return false;
    if (aircraft.production_end_year == null) return true;
    return year <= aircraft.production_end_year;
  }
  function canFlyDistance(aircraft, distanceKm2) {
    return distanceKm2 <= aircraft.range_km * 0.9;
  }
  function airportRunwayLengthM(airport) {
    if (airport?.waterOnly || airport?.type === "water_platform" || airport?.type === "seaplane_terminal") return null;
    if (airport?.runway_length_m) return airport.runway_length_m;
    if (airport?.max_runway_ft) return Math.round(airport.max_runway_ft * FT_TO_M);
    return null;
  }
  function canOperateAtAirport(aircraft, airport) {
    if (airport?.waterOnly || airport?.type === "water_platform" || airport?.type === "seaplane_terminal") {
      return aircraft?.waterCapable === true || aircraft?.supportedSurfaceTypes?.includes?.("water");
    }
    const runwayM = airportRunwayLengthM(airport);
    if (!runwayM) return true;
    return runwayM >= aircraft.min_runway_m;
  }
  function getAirportServiceScope(airport, year = null) {
    if (!airport) return "unknown";
    const timeline = airport.service_scope_timeline;
    if (year != null && Array.isArray(timeline)) {
      for (const entry of timeline) {
        const from = entry.from_year ?? -Infinity;
        const to = entry.to_year ?? Infinity;
        if (year >= from && year <= to) return entry.scope ?? airport.service_scope ?? "unknown";
      }
    }
    return airport.service_scope ?? "unknown";
  }
  function airportServiceScopeLabel(airport, year = null) {
    const scope = getAirportServiceScope(airport, year);
    return SERVICE_SCOPE_LABELS_KO[scope] ?? SERVICE_SCOPE_LABELS_KO.unknown;
  }
  function airportRouteScopeFailure(airport, otherAirport, distanceKm2, year = null) {
    if (!airport || !otherAirport) return "\uACF5\uD56D \uC815\uBCF4\uAC00 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.";
    const scope = getAirportServiceScope(airport, year);
    if (airport.service_scope_enforced !== false) {
      const isDomestic = airport.country === otherAirport.country;
      if (scope === "no_scheduled_service") {
        return `${airport.iata}(${airport.city ?? airport.name})\uB294 ${year ?? "\uD604\uC7AC"} \uC815\uAE30 \uBBFC\uD56D \uB178\uC120\uC744 \uCDE8\uAE09\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`;
      }

        return `${airport.iata}(${airport.city ?? airport.name})\uB294 ${year ?? "\uD604\uC7AC"} \uAD6D\uB0B4\uC120 \uC804\uC6A9 \uACF5\uD56D\uC785\uB2C8\uB2E4.`;
      }

      }

    return null;

  function routeOperationFailureReason(aircraft, airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year = null) {
    const scopeA = airportRouteScopeFailure(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year);
    if (scopeA) return scopeA;
    const scopeB = airportRouteScopeFailure(airportB, airportA, distanceKm2, year);
    if (scopeB) return scopeB;
    const waterNodes=[airportA,airportB].filter(x=>x?.waterOnly || x?.type === "water_platform" || x?.type === "seaplane_terminal");
    if (waterNodes.length && !(aircraft?.waterCapable === true || aircraft?.supportedSurfaceTypes?.includes?.("water"))) {
      return `${aircraft.model}은(는) 수상기장 운항형이 아닙니다.`;
    }
    if (!canFlyDistance(aircraft, distanceKm2)) {
      return `${aircraft.model}\uC758 \uC548\uC804 \uC6B4\uD56D\uAC70\uB9AC(${Math.round(aircraft.range_km * 0.9).toLocaleString()}km)\uAC00 \uB178\uC120 \uAC70\uB9AC ${Math.round(distanceKm2).toLocaleString()}km\uBCF4\uB2E4 \uC9E7\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`;
    }
    for (const airport of [airportA, airportB]) {
      const runwayM = airportRunwayLengthM(airport);
      if (runwayM && runwayM < aircraft.min_runway_m) {

      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  function compatibleAircraftAtAirport(aircraftList, airport, year = null) {
    return aircraftList.filter((aircraft) => (year == null || isAircraftAvailable(aircraft, year)) && canOperateAtAirport(aircraft, airport)).sort((a, b) => (b.seats ?? 0) - (a.seats ?? 0) || (b.range_km ?? 0) - (a.range_km ?? 0));
  }
  function canOperateRoute(aircraft, airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year = null) {

  function violatesSecondaryAirportRestriction(airport, otherAirport, distanceKm2, year) {
    if (year != null && airport.restriction_start_year != null && year < airport.restriction_start_year) {

  }
  function estimateFlightHours(aircraft, distanceKm2) {

  function weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(aircraft, distanceKm2, flightsPerWeekOneWay, officialFlightTimeMin = null) {
    const hasOfficialFlightTime = officialFlightTimeMin != null && officialFlightTimeMin !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(officialFlightTimeMin));
    const flightHoursOneWay = hasOfficialFlightTime ? Number(officialFlightTimeMin) / 60 : estimateFlightHours(aircraft, distanceKm2);
    const turnaround = aircraft.turnaround_hours ?? 1;
    const hoursPerRoundTrip = flightHoursOneWay * 2 + turnaround * 2;
    return hoursPerRoundTrip * flightsPerWeekOneWay;
  }

  function recommendMealTier(flightHoursOneWay) {

  }
  var CABIN_CLASSES = {
    economy: { fareMultiplier: 1, mealCostMultiplier: 1 },
    business: { fareMultiplier: 2.85, mealCostMultiplier: 2.5 },
    first: { fareMultiplier: 5.75, mealCostMultiplier: 4.5 }
  };
  function defaultCabinMix(aircraft) {
    if (aircraft.category === "supersonic") {
      return { economy: 0, business: 0.6, first: 0.4 };
    }
    if (aircraft.seats >= 250) {
      return { economy: 0.78, business: 0.18, first: 0.04 };
    }
    if (aircraft.seats >= 100) {
      return { economy: 0.88, business: 0.12, first: 0 };
    }
    return { economy: 1, business: 0, first: 0 };
  }
  function cabinWeightedSum(cabinMix, key) {
    return Object.entries(cabinMix).reduce(
      (sum, [cls, share]) => sum + share * (CABIN_CLASSES[cls]?.[key] ?? 1),
      0
    );
  }
  var SELF_BUILT_CATERING_PROFILE = { quality_tier: 3, price_multiplier: 0.65 };
  function mealCostPerPax(mealTierId, cabinMix, inHouseCatering, outsourceCateringCompany = null) {
    const tier = MEAL_TIERS[mealTierId] ?? MEAL_TIERS.none;
    const profile = inHouseCatering ? SELF_BUILT_CATERING_PROFILE : outsourceCateringCompany;
    const base = tier.costPerPaxOutsourced * (profile?.price_multiplier ?? 1);
    const classMultiplier = cabinWeightedSum(cabinMix, "mealCostMultiplier");
    return base * classMultiplier;
  }
  function serviceQualityBonus(mealTierId, cabinMix, outsourceCateringCompany = null, inHouseCatering = false) {
    const tier = MEAL_TIERS[mealTierId] ?? MEAL_TIERS.none;
    const premiumShare = (cabinMix.business ?? 0) + (cabinMix.first ?? 0);
    let bonus = tier.satisfactionScore + premiumShare * 0.15;
    const profile = inHouseCatering ? SELF_BUILT_CATERING_PROFILE : outsourceCateringCompany;
    if (profile) {
      bonus += (profile.quality_tier - 3) * 0.02;
    }
    return bonus;
  }

  var DEFAULT_TRAJECTORY = [[1955, 0.3], [1990, 0.55], [2020, 1], [2050, 1.15]];
  function interpolate(curve, year) {
    if (year <= curve[0][0]) return curve[0][1];
    const last = curve[curve.length - 1];
    if (year >= last[0]) return last[1];
    for (let i = 0; i < curve.length - 1; i++) {
      const [y0, v0] = curve[i];
      const [y1, v1] = curve[i + 1];
      if (year >= y0 && year <= y1) {
        const t = (year - y0) / (y1 - y0);
        return v0 + t * (v1 - v0);
      }
    }
    return 1;
  }
  function countryEconomyMultiplier(country, year, gameState2) {
    const curve = COUNTRY_TRAJECTORIES[country] ?? DEFAULT_TRAJECTORY;
    let value = interpolate(curve, year);
    const modifier = gameState2?.countryEconomicModifiers?.[country];
    if (modifier) value *= modifier;
    return value;
  }
  function setCountryEconomicModifier(gameState2, country, multiplier) {
    gameState2.countryEconomicModifiers ?? (gameState2.countryEconomicModifiers = {});
    gameState2.countryEconomicModifiers[country] = multiplier;
  }

  var MARKET_SIZE_BY_TIER = { 5: 100, 4: 50, 3: 20, 2: 6, 1: 2 };
  function marketSize(airport, tourismLevel = 0, year = null, gameState2 = null) {
    const base = MARKET_SIZE_BY_TIER[airport.hub_tier] ?? 20;
    const tourismFactor = 1 + 0.08 * tourismLevel;
    const econFactor = year != null ? countryEconomyMultiplier(airport.country, year, gameState2) : 1;
    return base * tourismFactor * econFactor;
  }
  var GROWTH_CURVE = [
    [1955, 0.04],
    [1965, 0.1],
    [1975, 0.18],
    [1985, 0.3],
    [1995, 0.5],
    [2005, 0.72],
    [2015, 0.9],
    [2020, 1],
    [2030, 1.25],
    [2040, 1.5],
    [2050, 1.75]
  ];
  function growthMultiplier(year) {
    if (year <= GROWTH_CURVE[0][0]) return GROWTH_CURVE[0][1];
    const last = GROWTH_CURVE[GROWTH_CURVE.length - 1];
    if (year >= last[0]) return last[1];
    for (let i = 0; i < GROWTH_CURVE.length - 1; i++) {
      const [y0, v0] = GROWTH_CURVE[i];
      const [y1, v1] = GROWTH_CURVE[i + 1];
      if (year >= y0 && year <= y1) {
        const t = (year - y0) / (y1 - y0);
        return v0 + t * (v1 - v0);
      }
    }
    return 1;
  }
  var GRAVITY_CONSTANT = 3.2;
  var DISTANCE_DECAY = 0.78;
  function interpolateCurve(curve, x) {
    if (x <= curve[0][0]) return curve[0][1];
    const last = curve[curve.length - 1];
    if (x >= last[0]) return last[1];
    for (let i = 0; i < curve.length - 1; i++) {
      const [x0, y0] = curve[i];
      const [x1, y1] = curve[i + 1];
      if (x >= x0 && x <= x1) {
        const t = (x - x0) / (x1 - x0);
        return y0 + t * (y1 - y0);
      }
    }
    return last[1];
  }
  function groundTransportDampening(distanceKm2) {
    if (distanceKm2 >= 400) return 1;
    if (distanceKm2 <= 80) return 0.5;
    return 0.5 + (distanceKm2 - 80) / (400 - 80) * (1 - 0.5);
  }
  var HSR_MATURE_YEAR = {
    JP: 1975,
    // 도카이도(1964)+산요신칸센(1972,오사카-오카야마/1975,~후쿠오카)로 서일본 주요 구간 커버
    FR: 1990,
    // TGV 파리-리옹(1981) 이후 남동/대서양 노선 확장
    DE: 2010,
    // ICE(1991) 이후 점진 확장, 뮌헨-베를린 등 주요 구간은 2017년에야 완전경쟁력 확보
    ES: 2008,
    // 마드리드-바르셀로나 완전개통(2008) 기준
    KR: 2010,
    // KTX(2004) 이후 호남선 등 확장
    CN: 2011,
    // 베이징-상하이 고속철 개통(2011.6) — 중국은 건설 속도가 이례적으로 빨라(2008년 이후
    // 8년 만에 세계 최대 고속철도망 완성) 다른 나라보다 훨씬 이르게 성숙기에 도달함
    IT: 2015,
    // 프레차로사 전국망 성숙기
    TR: 2016
    // 앙카라-이스탄불 등
  };
  var HSR_INTENSITY = {
    KR: 0.6,
    FR: 0.85,
    DE: 0.8,
    IT: 0.9,
    TR: 0.6
    // ES는 1.0(기본값)과 사실상 동일해 별도 항목 불필요
  };
  var HSR_DISTANCE_SCALE = {
    CN: 2.7
    // 최고 350km/h급 고속철로 항공이 경쟁을 포기하는 거리 자체가 훨씬 멀리까지 확장됨
    // (베이징-상하이 1250km에서도 "항공이 이 시장에서 사실상 손을 뗐다"는 학술논문 근거로 캘리브레이션)
  };
  var HSR_MATURE_AIR_SHARE_CURVE = [
    [0, 0],
    [150, 0],
    [460, 0],
    [515, 0.19],
    [650, 0.3],
    [820, 0.32],
    [950, 0.55],
    [1069, 0.93],
    [1300, 1],
    [2e3, 1]
  ];
  function hsrDampening(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year) {
    const scale = HSR_DISTANCE_SCALE[airportA.country] ?? 1;
    if (distanceKm2 > 1300 * scale) return 1;
    if (airportA.country !== airportB.country) return 1;
    const matureYear = HSR_MATURE_YEAR[airportA.country];
    if (!matureYear || year < matureYear) return 1;
    const yearsSinceMature = year - matureYear;
    const maturityStrength = Math.min(1, yearsSinceMature / 10);
    const intensity = HSR_INTENSITY[airportA.country] ?? 1;
    const matureAirShare = interpolateCurve(HSR_MATURE_AIR_SHARE_CURVE, distanceKm2 / scale);
    return 1 - maturityStrength * intensity * (1 - matureAirShare);
  }
  var MIN_COMBINED_SCARCITY = 0.06;
  function metroShare(airport) {
    return airport?.metro_share ?? 1;
  }
  function metroId(airport) {
    return airport?.metro_id ?? airport?.metro_group ?? `${airport?.country ?? "XX"}_${airport?.city ?? airport?.iata ?? "unknown"}`;
  }
  function airportIsActiveAtYear(airport, year) {
    if (!airport) return false;
    if (airport.opened_year && year < airport.opened_year) return false;
    if (airport.closed_year && year >= airport.closed_year) return false;
    return true;
  }
  function scopeAtYear(airport, year) {
    for (const period of airport?.service_scope_timeline ?? []) {
      const from = period.from_year ?? -Infinity;
      const to = period.to_year ?? Infinity;
      if (year >= from && year <= to) return period.scope;
    }
    return airport?.service_scope ?? "both";
  }
  function airportRoleAtYear(airport, year) {
    if (!airportIsActiveAtYear(airport, year)) return "inactive";
    for (const period of airport?.airport_role_history ?? []) {
      const from = period.from_year ?? -Infinity;
      const to = period.to_year ?? Infinity;
      if (year >= from && year <= to) return period.role;
    }
    const scope = scopeAtYear(airport, year);
    if (scope === "no_scheduled_service") return "inactive";
    if (scope === "domestic_only") return "domestic_gateway";
    return airport?.airport_role ?? (airport?.hub_tier >= 4 ? "primary_gateway" : "regional_airport");
  }
  function transferConvenienceIndex(airport, year) {
    if (!airportIsActiveAtYear(airport, year) || airportRoleAtYear(airport, year) === "inactive") return 0;
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, airport?.transfer_convenience_index ?? 0.55));
  }
  var METRO_STATIC_CACHE = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakMap();
  function metroStaticContext(airportsByIata2) {
    if (!airportsByIata2) return null;
    let cached = METRO_STATIC_CACHE.get(airportsByIata2);
    if (cached) return cached;
    const groups = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const airport of Object.values(airportsByIata2)) {
      const id = metroId(airport);
      if (!groups.has(id)) groups.set(id, []);
      groups.get(id).push(airport);
    }
    cached = { groups };
    METRO_STATIC_CACHE.set(airportsByIata2, cached);
    return cached;
  }
  function activeMetroMembers(airport, year, airportsByIata2) {
    const ctx = metroStaticContext(airportsByIata2);
    if (!ctx) return airportIsActiveAtYear(airport, year) ? [airport] : [];
    return (ctx.groups.get(metroId(airport)) ?? [airport]).filter((a) => airportIsActiveAtYear(a, year) && airportRoleAtYear(a, year) !== "inactive");
  }
  function airportBaseTierSize(airport) {
    return MARKET_SIZE_BY_TIER[airport?.hub_tier] ?? 20;
  }
  function metroProfile(airport, year, gameState2, airportsByIata2) {
    const members = activeMetroMembers(airport, year, airportsByIata2);
    if (members.length === 0) return { members: [], size: 0, premium: 0.5, tourism: 0, lat: airport?.lat ?? 0, lon: airport?.lon ?? 0 };
    const sizes = members.map(airportBaseTierSize).sort((a, b) => b - a);
    const maxSize = sizes[0] ?? 20;
    const secondaryContribution = sizes.slice(1).reduce((sum, v) => sum + v * 0.16, 0);
    const baseSize = Math.min(maxSize * 1.38, maxSize + secondaryContribution);
    const tourism = Math.max(0, ...members.map((a) => gameState2?.cityTourismLevel?.[a.iata] ?? 0));
    const tourismFactor = 1 + 0.08 * tourism;
    let weightSum = 0, premiumSum = 0, latSum = 0, lonSum = 0;
    for (const a of members) {
      const w = airportBaseTierSize(a);
      weightSum += w;
      premiumSum += (a.premium_demand_index ?? 0.5) * w;
      latSum += (a.lat ?? 0) * w;
      lonSum += (a.lon ?? 0) * w;
    }
    return {
      members,
      size: baseSize * tourismFactor,
      premium: weightSum > 0 ? premiumSum / weightSum : 0.5,
      tourism,
      lat: weightSum > 0 ? latSum / weightSum : airport.lat,
      lon: weightSum > 0 ? lonSum / weightSum : airport.lon
    };
  }
  function coordDistanceKm(a, b) {
    const toRad2 = (d) => d * Math.PI / 180;
    const p1 = toRad2(a.lat), p2 = toRad2(b.lat);
    const dp = toRad2(b.lat - a.lat), dl = toRad2(b.lon - a.lon);
    const h = Math.sin(dp / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(p1) * Math.cos(p2) * Math.sin(dl / 2) ** 2;
    return 6371 * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(h), Math.sqrt(1 - h));
  }
  var ROLE_LOCAL_FACTOR = {
    primary_gateway: 1,
    transfer_hub: 0.95,
    domestic_gateway: 0.92,
    secondary_airport: 0.7,
    regional_airport: 0.58,
    limited_civil: 0.24,
    inactive: 0
  };
  function airportSelectionWeight(airport, year, purpose = "leisure", { isInternational = false, transfer = false } = {}) {
    if (!airportIsActiveAtYear(airport, year)) return 0;
    const role = airportRoleAtYear(airport, year);
    if (role === "inactive") return 0;
    const access = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(1, airport?.city_access_index ?? 0.65));
    const accessExponent = purpose === "business" ? 1.55 : purpose === "vfr_essential" ? 1.05 : 0.75;
    let roleFactor = ROLE_LOCAL_FACTOR[role] ?? 0.6;
    if (role === "domestic_gateway" && isInternational) roleFactor *= 0.38;
    if (role === "primary_gateway" && !isInternational) roleFactor *= 0.94;
    if (transfer) {
      roleFactor *= 0.55 + 0.65 * transferConvenienceIndex(airport, year);
    }
    return Math.max(0, access ** accessExponent * roleFactor);
  }
  function endpointShare(airport, profile, year, purpose, isInternational) {
    const own = airportSelectionWeight(airport, year, purpose, { isInternational });
    const denom = profile.members.reduce((sum, a) => sum + airportSelectionWeight(a, year, purpose, { isInternational }), 0);
    return denom > 0 ? own / denom : 0;
  }
  function massTourismMaturity(year) {
    if (year <= 1955) return 0.08;
    if (year >= 2020) return 1;
    return 0.08 + (year - 1955) / 65 * 0.92;
  }
  function demandTypeShares(profileA, profileB, year, isDomestic) {
    const premium = (profileA.premium + profileB.premium) / 2;
    const tourism = (profileA.tourism + profileB.tourism) / 2;
    const maturity = massTourismMaturity(year);
    const businessRaw = 0.55 + premium * 1.05 + (1 - maturity) * 0.38;
    const leisureRaw = 0.28 + (1 - premium) * 0.58 + maturity * 0.88 + tourism * 0.1;
    const vfrRaw = (isDomestic ? 0.78 : 0.46) + (1 - premium) * 0.18;
    const sum = businessRaw + leisureRaw + vfrRaw;
    return {
      business: businessRaw / sum,
      leisure: leisureRaw / sum,
      vfr_essential: vfrRaw / sum
    };
  }
  function estimateWeeklyDemandBreakdown(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year, tourismLevelA = 0, tourismLevelB = 0, gameState2 = null, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 = null } = {}) {
    if (!airportsByIata2) {
      const total2 = estimateWeeklyDemandLegacy(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year, tourismLevelA, tourismLevelB, gameState2);
      const premium = ((airportA?.premium_demand_index ?? 0.5) + (airportB?.premium_demand_index ?? 0.5)) / 2;
      const shares2 = demandTypeShares(
        { premium, tourism: tourismLevelA },
        { premium, tourism: tourismLevelB },
        year,
        airportA?.country === airportB?.country
      );
      return { total: total2, metroTotal: total2, byType: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(shares2).map(([k, v]) => [k, total2 * v])), metroByType: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(shares2).map(([k, v]) => [k, total2 * v])), shares: shares2 };
    }
    if (!airportIsActiveAtYear(airportA, year) || !airportIsActiveAtYear(airportB, year)) {
      return { total: 0, metroTotal: 0, byType: { business: 0, leisure: 0, vfr_essential: 0 }, metroByType: { business: 0, leisure: 0, vfr_essential: 0 }, shares: { business: 0, leisure: 0, vfr_essential: 0 } };
    }
    const profileA = metroProfile(airportA, year, gameState2, airportsByIata2);
    const profileB = metroProfile(airportB, year, gameState2, airportsByIata2);
    const rawScarcity = countryEconomyMultiplier(airportA.country, year, gameState2) * countryEconomyMultiplier(airportB.country, year, gameState2) * costEraMultiplier(year);
    const scarcity = Math.max(MIN_COMBINED_SCARCITY, rawScarcity);
    const metroDistance = Math.max(1, coordDistanceKm(profileA, profileB));
    const base = GRAVITY_CONSTANT * profileA.size * profileB.size / Math.pow(1 + metroDistance / 2e3, DISTANCE_DECAY);
    const metroTotal = base * scarcity * groundTransportDampening(metroDistance) * hsrDampening(airportA, airportB, metroDistance, year);
    const isInternational = airportA.country !== airportB.country;
    const shares = demandTypeShares(profileA, profileB, year, !isInternational);
    const metroByType = {};
    const byType = {};
    for (const purpose of ["business", "leisure", "vfr_essential"]) {
      metroByType[purpose] = metroTotal * shares[purpose];
      const shareA = endpointShare(airportA, profileA, year, purpose, isInternational);
      const shareB = endpointShare(airportB, profileB, year, purpose, isInternational);
      byType[purpose] = metroByType[purpose] * shareA * shareB;
    }
    const total = Object.values(byType).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    return { total, metroTotal, byType, metroByType, shares, metroA: metroId(airportA), metroB: metroId(airportB), metroDistance };
  }
  function estimateWeeklyDemandLegacy(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year, tourismLevelA = 0, tourismLevelB = 0, gameState2 = null) {
    const tierSizeA = (MARKET_SIZE_BY_TIER[airportA.hub_tier] ?? 20) * (1 + 0.08 * tourismLevelA);
    const tierSizeB = (MARKET_SIZE_BY_TIER[airportB.hub_tier] ?? 20) * (1 + 0.08 * tourismLevelB);
    const rawScarcity = countryEconomyMultiplier(airportA.country, year, gameState2) * countryEconomyMultiplier(airportB.country, year, gameState2) * costEraMultiplier(year);
    const scarcity = Math.max(MIN_COMBINED_SCARCITY, rawScarcity);
    const base = GRAVITY_CONSTANT * tierSizeA * tierSizeB / Math.pow(1 + distanceKm2 / 2e3, DISTANCE_DECAY);
    return base * scarcity * groundTransportDampening(distanceKm2) * hsrDampening(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year) * metroShare(airportA) * metroShare(airportB);
  }
  function premiumDemandIndex(airport) {
    return airport?.premium_demand_index ?? 0.5;
  }
  function routePremiumDemandIndex(airportA, airportB) {
    return (premiumDemandIndex(airportA) + premiumDemandIndex(airportB)) / 2;
  }
  var CLASS_DEMAND_SENSITIVITY = { economy: -1, business: 1.6, first: 1.8 };
  function classDemandBias(cls, routePremiumIndex = 0.5) {
    const delta = routePremiumIndex - 0.5;
    const raw = 1 + delta * (CLASS_DEMAND_SENSITIVITY[cls] ?? 0);
    return Math.max(0.35, Math.min(1.9, raw));
  }
  function suggestCabinMix(aircraft, airportA, airportB) {
    const base = defaultCabinMix(aircraft);
    const basePremium = (base.business ?? 0) + (base.first ?? 0);
    if (basePremium <= 0) return base;
    const delta = routePremiumDemandIndex(airportA, airportB) - 0.5;
    const premiumScale = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(1.6, 1 + delta * 1.2));
    let business = (base.business ?? 0) * premiumScale;
    let first = (base.first ?? 0) * premiumScale;
    const premiumTotal = business + first;
    if (premiumTotal > 0.9) {
      const scaleDown = 0.9 / premiumTotal;
      business *= scaleDown;
      first *= scaleDown;
    }
    return { economy: 1 - business - first, business, first };
  }
  function baseFarePerKm(distanceKm2) {
    return 0.34 / Math.pow(1 + distanceKm2 / 1500, 0.35);
  }
  function blendFareMultiplier(fareMultiplier, cabinMix) {
    if (typeof fareMultiplier === "number") return fareMultiplier;
    const c = cabinMix ?? { economy: 1 };
    return (c.economy ?? 0) * (fareMultiplier.economy ?? 1) + (c.business ?? 0) * (fareMultiplier.business ?? 1) + (c.first ?? 0) * (fareMultiplier.first ?? 1);
  }
  function classFareMultiplier(fareMultiplier, cls) {
    return typeof fareMultiplier === "number" ? fareMultiplier : fareMultiplier[cls] ?? 1;
  }
  function computeLoadFactor({ fareMultiplier = 1, competitorCount = 0, serviceBonus = 0, demandBias = 1 }) {
    const priceEffect = Math.pow(1 / fareMultiplier, 0.6);
    const competitionEffect = 1 / (1 + 0.18 * competitorCount);
    const raw = 0.82 * priceEffect * competitionEffect * (1 + serviceBonus) * demandBias;
    return Math.max(0.15, Math.min(0.98, raw));
  }
  function costEraMultiplier(year) {
    return Math.max(0.25, growthMultiplier(year));
  }
  function landingFeeUsd(airport, year = null, gameState2 = null, aircraft = null) {
    const base = 400 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 350;
    const sizeFactor = aircraft ? Math.max(0.15, Math.min(1.85, aircraft.isFreighter ? ((aircraft.payload_kg ?? aircraft.cargo_kg ?? 15000) / 45000) : ((aircraft.seats ?? 0) / 150))) : 1;
    const sized = base * sizeFactor;
    if (year == null) return sized;
    const countryFactor = Math.max(0.25, countryEconomyMultiplier(airport.country, year, gameState2));
    return sized * countryFactor;
  }

  var CARGO_GRAVITY_CONSTANT = 0.0092;

  var CARGO_RATE_PER_KG_KM_BASE = 105e-5;
  var CARGO_CLASSES_V600 = {
    general: { label: "일반화물", densityKgM3: 165, yieldMultiplier: 1.00, handlingUsdKg: 0.16, priceElasticity: 0.42 },
    express: { label: "특송화물", densityKgM3: 95, yieldMultiplier: 1.78, handlingUsdKg: 0.27, priceElasticity: 0.26 },
    special: { label: "특수화물", densityKgM3: 220, yieldMultiplier: 2.20, handlingUsdKg: 0.43, priceElasticity: 0.18 }
  };
  var CARGO_HUB_MULTIPLIER = {
    QA: [[1955, 1], [1985, 1], [1995, 1.3], [2005, 2.2], [2015, 3.5], [2024, 4], [2050, 4]],
    AE: [[1955, 1], [1985, 1], [1995, 1.4], [2005, 2], [2015, 3], [2024, 3.5], [2050, 3.5]],
    HK: [[1955, 1], [1970, 1.3], [1990, 1.8], [2010, 2.2], [2024, 2.3], [2050, 2.3]],
    SG: [[1955, 1], [1965, 1.1], [1990, 1.6], [2010, 2], [2024, 2.1], [2050, 2.1]],
    KR: [[1955, 1], [1988, 1.1], [2001, 1.3], [2010, 1.4], [2024, 1.5], [2050, 1.5]],
    DE: [[1955, 1.1], [1990, 1.2], [2024, 1.4], [2050, 1.4]],
    JP: [[1955, 1], [1965, 1.4], [1985, 1.7], [2e3, 1.4], [2024, 1.1], [2050, 1.1]],
    ET: [[1955, 1], [1990, 1], [2005, 1.3], [2015, 1.8], [2024, 2.2], [2050, 2.2]],
    LU: [[1955, 1], [1970, 1.3], [1990, 1.8], [2010, 2.3], [2024, 2.4], [2050, 2.4]],
    NL: [[1955, 1.1], [1990, 1.3], [2024, 1.4], [2050, 1.4]],
    TR: [[1955, 1], [1995, 1], [2010, 1.3], [2020, 1.7], [2024, 1.8], [2050, 1.8]]
  };
  var CARGO_EXPORT_INDEX_V600 = { CN:1.85, HK:1.60, DE:1.55, KR:1.52, JP:1.45, SG:1.45, TW:1.52, US:1.28, NL:1.35, BE:1.28, FR:1.20, GB:1.12, IT:1.18, TR:1.20, AE:1.18, QA:1.08, IN:1.24, VN:1.32, TH:1.20, MY:1.26, MX:1.26, BR:1.16, CA:1.12, AU:1.08, ET:1.05, KE:1.04, LU:1.28, SU:1.16, RU:1.12 };
  var CARGO_IMPORT_INDEX_V600 = { US:1.62, CN:1.32, HK:1.38, DE:1.35, GB:1.30, FR:1.28, JP:1.25, KR:1.20, SG:1.34, AE:1.40, QA:1.28, CA:1.24, AU:1.22, NL:1.30, BE:1.25, IT:1.20, ES:1.18, IN:1.15, BR:1.12, MX:1.16, TR:1.17, SA:1.28, LU:1.22, SU:1.08, RU:1.10 };
  function isFreighterAircraftV600(aircraft) { return Boolean(aircraft?.isFreighter || aircraft?.category === "freighter" || (aircraft?.seats ?? 0) === 0 && (aircraft?.payload_kg ?? 0) > 0); }
  function aircraftRoleCompatibleV600(aircraft, routeType="passenger") {
    return routeType === "cargo" ? isFreighterAircraftV600(aircraft) : !isFreighterAircraftV600(aircraft);
  }
  function cargoGroundCompetitionDampening(distanceKm2) {
    if (distanceKm2 >= 1500) return 1;
    if (distanceKm2 <= 300) return 0.15;
    return 0.15 + (distanceKm2 - 300) / 1200 * 0.85;
  }
  function cargoEraMultiplier(year) {
    if (year <= 1960) return 0.26;
    if (year <= 1990) return 0.26 + (year - 1960) / 30 * 0.31;
    if (year >= 2020) return 1.3;
    return 0.57 + (year - 1990) / 30 * 0.73;
  }
  // alpha6.0-b1: calibrates the airport-pair market represented by one game route.
  // The foundation formula compounded economy and era factors too strongly in early years,
  // producing ~1% load factors for viable 1970 all-cargo routes.
  function cargoMarketCalibrationV600B1(year) {
    return interpolateCurve([[1955,48],[1970,40],[1985,20],[2000,7.5],[2010,4],[2025,2.5],[2050,2.5]], year);
  }
  function cargoHubMultiplier(country, year) {
    const traj = CARGO_HUB_MULTIPLIER[country];
    return traj ? interpolateCurve(traj, year) : 1;
  }
  function cargoClassSharesV600(year) {
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (year - 1960) / 65));
    const express = 0.05 + t * 0.24;
    const special = 0.13 + t * 0.02;
    return { general: 1 - express - special, express, special };
  }
  function cargoAirportIndexesV600(airport) {
    const tier = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, airport?.hub_tier ?? 2));
    const derived = 0.55 + tier * 0.11;
    return {
      gateway: Math.max(0.35, airport?.cargo_gateway_index ?? derived),
      warehouse: Math.max(0.35, airport?.warehouse_capacity_index ?? (0.52 + tier * 0.105)),
      customs: Math.max(0.45, airport?.customs_efficiency_index ?? (0.62 + tier * 0.075)),
      night: Math.max(0.45, airport?.night_operation_index ?? (0.70 + tier * 0.055)),
      express: Math.max(0.35, airport?.express_hub_index ?? (0.48 + tier * 0.105)),
      catchment: Math.max(0.40, airport?.cargo_catchment_index ?? (0.55 + tier * 0.095))
    };
  }
  function cargoDirectionalTradeFactorV600(originCountry, destinationCountry) {
    if (originCountry === destinationCountry) return 0.78;
    const exportIndex = CARGO_EXPORT_INDEX_V600[originCountry] ?? 1;
    const importIndex = CARGO_IMPORT_INDEX_V600[destinationCountry] ?? 1;
    return Math.sqrt(exportIndex * importIndex);
  }
  function cargoYieldPerKg(distanceKm2) {
    const ratePerKgKm = CARGO_RATE_PER_KG_KM_BASE / Math.pow(1 + distanceKm2 / 1500, 0.35);
    return distanceKm2 * ratePerKgKm;
  }
  function estimateWeeklyCargoDemandBreakdownV600(origin, destination, distanceKm2, year, gameState2 = null) {
    const sizeOrigin = MARKET_SIZE_BY_TIER[origin.hub_tier] ?? 20;
    const sizeDestination = MARKET_SIZE_BY_TIER[destination.hub_tier] ?? 20;
    const economy = Math.max(MIN_COMBINED_SCARCITY, Math.sqrt(
      countryEconomyMultiplier(origin.country, year, gameState2) * countryEconomyMultiplier(destination.country, year, gameState2)
    ) * costEraMultiplier(year));
    const originIndex = cargoAirportIndexesV600(origin);
    const destinationIndex = cargoAirportIndexesV600(destination);
    const gateway = Math.sqrt(originIndex.gateway * destinationIndex.gateway * originIndex.catchment * destinationIndex.catchment);
    const hubBoost = Math.sqrt(cargoHubMultiplier(origin.country, year) * cargoHubMultiplier(destination.country, year));
    const directionalTrade = cargoDirectionalTradeFactorV600(origin.country, destination.country);
    const base = CARGO_GRAVITY_CONSTANT * sizeOrigin * sizeDestination * 1000;
    const total = base * economy * cargoEraMultiplier(year) * cargoMarketCalibrationV600B1(year) * cargoGroundCompetitionDampening(distanceKm2) * gateway * hubBoost * directionalTrade * metroShare(origin) * metroShare(destination);
    const shares = cargoClassSharesV600(year);
    const expressBoost = Math.sqrt(originIndex.express * destinationIndex.express * originIndex.night * destinationIndex.night);
    const specialBoost = Math.sqrt(originIndex.customs * destinationIndex.customs * originIndex.warehouse * destinationIndex.warehouse);
    const raw = {
      general: total * shares.general,
      express: total * shares.express * expressBoost,
      special: total * shares.special * specialBoost
    };
    const rawTotal = Object.values(raw).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    return { totalKg: rawTotal, byClass: raw, direction: `${origin.iata}->${destination.iata}`, tradeFactor: directionalTrade, airportFactor: gateway };
  }
  function estimateWeeklyCargoDemandKg(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year, gameState2 = null) {
    const ab = estimateWeeklyCargoDemandBreakdownV600(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year, gameState2).totalKg;
    const ba = estimateWeeklyCargoDemandBreakdownV600(airportB, airportA, distanceKm2, year, gameState2).totalKg;
    return ab + ba;
  }
  function payloadAtDistanceV600(aircraft, distanceKm2) {
    const structural = Math.max(0, aircraft.payload_kg ?? aircraft.cargo_kg ?? 0);
    const maxRangePayload = Math.max(1, aircraft.range_at_max_payload_km ?? aircraft.range_km * 0.62);
    const maxRange = Math.max(maxRangePayload, aircraft.range_km ?? maxRangePayload);
    if (distanceKm2 <= maxRangePayload) return structural;
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (distanceKm2 - maxRangePayload) / Math.max(1, maxRange - maxRangePayload)));
    return structural * Math.max(0.35, 1 - 0.65 * t);
  }
  function cargoCapacityPerFlightV600(aircraft, distanceKm2, passengerLoadFactor=0) {
    if (isFreighterAircraftV600(aircraft)) {
      return { weightKg: payloadAtDistanceV600(aircraft, distanceKm2), volumeM3: Math.max(1, aircraft.cargo_volume_m3 ?? (aircraft.payload_kg ?? aircraft.cargo_kg ?? 0) / 155), source: "main_deck_freighter" };
    }
    const seats = Math.max(0, aircraft.seats ?? 0);
    const nominalBelly = Math.max(0, aircraft.cargo_kg ?? 0);
    const structuralPayload = Math.max(nominalBelly, aircraft.max_payload_kg ?? seats * 105 + nominalBelly * 1.18);
    const rangeRatio = distanceKm2 / Math.max(1, aircraft.range_km ?? distanceKm2);
    const rangePayloadFactor = rangeRatio <= 0.55 ? 1 : Math.max(0.68, 1 - (rangeRatio - 0.55) / 0.35 * 0.32);
    const passengersPerFlight = seats * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, passengerLoadFactor));
    const weightAvailable = Math.max(0, structuralPayload * rangePayloadFactor - passengersPerFlight * 105);
    const lowLoadFlex = 1 + (1 - Math.max(0, Math.min(1, passengerLoadFactor))) * 0.30;
    const weightKg = Math.max(0, Math.min(weightAvailable, nominalBelly * lowLoadFlex));
    const volumeM3 = Math.max(0, aircraft.belly_volume_m3 ?? nominalBelly / 145 * 1.08);
    return { weightKg, volumeM3, source: "passenger_belly" };
  }
  function cargoDemandEventMultiplierV600(cls, multiplier) {
    const m = Math.max(0.05, Number(multiplier) || 1);
    if (cls === "express" && m < 0.8) return Math.max(0.75, Math.pow(m, 0.18) * 1.08);
    if (cls === "special") return Math.pow(m, 0.38);
    return Math.pow(m, 0.58);
  }
  function allocateCargoDirectionV600({ demand, capacity, distanceKm2, cargoFareMultiplier, competitorCount, airportA, airportB, year, demandMultiplier=1 }) {
    const effective = {};
    for (const [cls, def] of Object.entries(CARGO_CLASSES_V600)) {
      const priceResponse = Math.pow(1 / Math.max(0.3, cargoFareMultiplier), def.priceElasticity);
      const competitionShare = 1 / (1 + 0.75 * Math.max(0, competitorCount));
      effective[cls] = Math.max(0, (demand.byClass?.[cls] ?? 0) * priceResponse * competitionShare * cargoDemandEventMultiplierV600(cls, demandMultiplier));
    }
    const totalWeightDemand = Object.values(effective).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    const totalVolumeDemand = Object.entries(effective).reduce((sum,[cls,kg]) => sum + kg / CARGO_CLASSES_V600[cls].densityKgM3, 0);
    const scale = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, CARGO_MAX_LOAD_FACTOR, capacity.weightKg > 0 ? capacity.weightKg * CARGO_MAX_LOAD_FACTOR / Math.max(1,totalWeightDemand) : 0, capacity.volumeM3 > 0 ? capacity.volumeM3 * CARGO_MAX_LOAD_FACTOR / Math.max(0.001,totalVolumeDemand) : 0));
    const carriedByClass = {};
    let carriedKg=0, usedVolumeM3=0, revenue=0, handlingCost=0;
    const handlingFactor = Math.sqrt(cargoAirportIndexesV600(airportA).warehouse * cargoAirportIndexesV600(airportB).warehouse);
    for (const [cls, def] of Object.entries(CARGO_CLASSES_V600)) {
      const kg = effective[cls] * scale;
      carriedByClass[cls] = kg;
      carriedKg += kg;
      usedVolumeM3 += kg / def.densityKgM3;
      revenue += kg * cargoYieldPerKg(distanceKm2) * def.yieldMultiplier * cargoFareMultiplier;
      handlingCost += kg * def.handlingUsdKg * handlingFactor * costEraMultiplier(year);
    }
    const weightLf = capacity.weightKg > 0 ? carriedKg / capacity.weightKg : 0;
    const volumeLf = capacity.volumeM3 > 0 ? usedVolumeM3 / capacity.volumeM3 : 0;
    return { demandKg: totalWeightDemand, carriedKg, carriedByClass, usedVolumeM3, revenue, handlingCost, loadFactor: Math.max(weightLf, volumeLf), weightLoadFactor: weightLf, volumeLoadFactor: volumeLf };
  }
  function simulateCargoOperation({ airportA, airportB, distanceKm: distanceKm2, aircraft, flightsPerWeekOneWay, year, gameState: gameState2 = null, competitorCount = 0, isFreighterRoute = false, cargoFareMultiplier = 1, passengerLoadFactor = 0, cargoDemandMultiplier = 1 }) {
    const compatible = isFreighterRoute ? isFreighterAircraftV600(aircraft) : !isFreighterAircraftV600(aircraft);
    const perFlight = compatible ? cargoCapacityPerFlightV600(aircraft, distanceKm2, passengerLoadFactor) : { weightKg:0, volumeM3:0, source:"invalid_aircraft_role" };
    const weeklyDirectionCapacity = { weightKg: perFlight.weightKg * Math.max(0, flightsPerWeekOneWay), volumeM3: perFlight.volumeM3 * Math.max(0, flightsPerWeekOneWay), source: perFlight.source };
    if (weeklyDirectionCapacity.weightKg <= 0 || weeklyDirectionCapacity.volumeM3 <= 0) {
      return { weeklyCargoCapacityKg:0, weeklyCargoCapacityVolumeM3:0, weeklyCargoDemandKg:0, cargoLoadFactor:0, cargoWeightLoadFactor:0, cargoVolumeLoadFactor:0, cargoKgCarried:0, cargoVolumeM3Carried:0, weeklyCargoRevenue:0, weeklyCargoHandlingCost:0, cargoOutboundKg:0, cargoInboundKg:0, cargoClassBreakdown:{general:0,express:0,special:0}, cargoDirectionBreakdown:{}, invalidAircraftRoleV600:!compatible };
    }
    const demandAB = estimateWeeklyCargoDemandBreakdownV600(airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year, gameState2);
    const demandBA = estimateWeeklyCargoDemandBreakdownV600(airportB, airportA, distanceKm2, year, gameState2);
    const out = allocateCargoDirectionV600({ demand:demandAB, capacity:weeklyDirectionCapacity, distanceKm2, cargoFareMultiplier, competitorCount, airportA, airportB, year, demandMultiplier:cargoDemandMultiplier });
    const inbound = allocateCargoDirectionV600({ demand:demandBA, capacity:weeklyDirectionCapacity, distanceKm2, cargoFareMultiplier, competitorCount, airportA:airportB, airportB:airportA, year, demandMultiplier:cargoDemandMultiplier });
    const classBreakdown = Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(CARGO_CLASSES_V600).map(cls => [cls, (out.carriedByClass[cls]??0)+(inbound.carriedByClass[cls]??0)]));
    const totalCapacityKg = weeklyDirectionCapacity.weightKg * 2;
    const totalCapacityVolume = weeklyDirectionCapacity.volumeM3 * 2;
    const carried = out.carriedKg + inbound.carriedKg;
    const usedVolume = out.usedVolumeM3 + inbound.usedVolumeM3;
    return {
      weeklyCargoCapacityKg: totalCapacityKg,
      weeklyCargoCapacityVolumeM3: totalCapacityVolume,
      weeklyCargoDemandKg: out.demandKg + inbound.demandKg,
      cargoLoadFactor: Math.max(totalCapacityKg>0?carried/totalCapacityKg:0,totalCapacityVolume>0?usedVolume/totalCapacityVolume:0),
      cargoWeightLoadFactor: totalCapacityKg>0?carried/totalCapacityKg:0,
      cargoVolumeLoadFactor: totalCapacityVolume>0?usedVolume/totalCapacityVolume:0,
      cargoKgCarried: carried,
      cargoVolumeM3Carried: usedVolume,
      weeklyCargoRevenue: out.revenue + inbound.revenue,
      weeklyCargoHandlingCost: out.handlingCost + inbound.handlingCost,
      cargoOutboundKg: out.carriedKg,
      cargoInboundKg: inbound.carriedKg,
      cargoClassBreakdown: classBreakdown,
      cargoDirectionBreakdown: {
        outbound: { from:airportA.iata, to:airportB.iata, demandKg:out.demandKg, carriedKg:out.carriedKg, loadFactor:out.loadFactor, weightLoadFactor:out.weightLoadFactor, volumeLoadFactor:out.volumeLoadFactor, byClass:out.carriedByClass },
        inbound: { from:airportB.iata, to:airportA.iata, demandKg:inbound.demandKg, carriedKg:inbound.carriedKg, loadFactor:inbound.loadFactor, weightLoadFactor:inbound.weightLoadFactor, volumeLoadFactor:inbound.volumeLoadFactor, byClass:inbound.carriedByClass }
      },
      payloadKgPerFlightV600: perFlight.weightKg,
      cargoVolumeM3PerFlightV600: perFlight.volumeM3,
      capacitySourceV600: perFlight.source,
      invalidAircraftRoleV600: false
    };
  }
  function simulateCargoOnlyRoute({ airportA, airportB, distanceKm: distanceKm2, aircraft, flightsPerWeekOneWay, year, gameState: gameState2, competitorCount, costMultiplier, cargoFareMultiplier, demandMultiplier=1, overheadRatio = 0.68 }) {
    const cargo = simulateCargoOperation({ airportA, airportB, distanceKm: distanceKm2, aircraft, flightsPerWeekOneWay, year, gameState: gameState2, competitorCount, isFreighterRoute: true, cargoFareMultiplier, cargoDemandMultiplier:demandMultiplier });
    const flightHours = estimateFlightHours(aircraft, distanceKm2);
    const weeklyFlightHours = flightHours * flightsPerWeekOneWay * 2;
    const eraCostMult = costEraMultiplier(year);
    const weeklyFuelCrewMaint = cargo.invalidAircraftRoleV600 ? 0 : weeklyFlightHours * aircraft.opcost_usd_hr * costMultiplier * eraCostMult;
    const weeklyLandingFees = cargo.invalidAircraftRoleV600 ? 0 : (landingFeeUsd(airportA, year, gameState2, aircraft) + landingFeeUsd(airportB, year, gameState2, aircraft)) * flightsPerWeekOneWay * eraCostMult;
    const weeklySgaOverhead = cargo.weeklyCargoRevenue * Math.max(0.08, Math.min(0.30, overheadRatio));
    const weeklyOperatingCost = weeklyFuelCrewMaint + weeklyLandingFees + cargo.weeklyCargoHandlingCost + weeklySgaOverhead;
    const emptyCabin = { seats: 0, idealPax: 0, loadFactor: 0, farePerPax: 0, paxCarried: 0, revenue: 0 };
    return { distanceKm:distanceKm2, weeklyDemand:0, loadFactor:0, weeklySeatCapacity:0, paxCarried:0, farePerPax:0, cabinBreakdown:{economy:{...emptyCabin},business:{...emptyCabin},first:{...emptyCabin}}, mealTierId:"none", avgMealCostPerPax:0, weeklyMealCost:0, weeklyFuelCrewMaint, weeklyLandingFees, weeklyPassengerRevenue:0, ...cargo, weeklyRevenue:cargo.weeklyCargoRevenue, weeklyOperatingCost, weeklyProfit:cargo.weeklyCargoRevenue-weeklyOperatingCost, routeType:"cargo" };
  }
  function simulateRouteOperation({
    airportA,
    airportB,
    distanceKm: distanceKm2,
    aircraft,
    flightsPerWeekOneWay,
    fareMultiplier = 1,
    competitorCount = 0,
    year,
    cabinMix = defaultCabinMix(aircraft),
    mealTierId = recommendMealTier(estimateFlightHours(aircraft, distanceKm2)),
    inHouseCateringA = false,
    inHouseCateringB = false,
    cateringCompanyA = null,
    // 노선 출발지 계약 업체 (data/catering.json, ancillary.js의 contractCatering 참고)
    cateringCompanyB = null,
    // 노선 도착지 계약 업체
    tourismLevelA = 0,
    tourismLevelB = 0,
    gameState: gameState2 = null,
    // countryEconomy.js 국가 경제력 배수 계산용(선택)
    airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 = null,
    // v1.9 도시권 O&D 배분용 전체 공항 인덱스
    extraDemand = 0,
    // 하위호환 숫자형 환승 보너스
    selfConnectingDemand = null,
    // {business, leisure, vfr_essential}
    allianceConnectingDemand = null,
    // {business, leisure, vfr_essential}
    localOdMultiplier = 1,
    // 운수권에 따른 현지 O&D 허용 배수(제5·8자유 through-only는 0)
    demandMultiplier = 1,
    // events.js의 수요 보정(경기침체/특수/불황 등)
    costMultiplier = 1,
    // events.js의 비용 보정(유가 급등 등)
    routeType = "passenger",
    // "passenger"(기본, 벨리카고 자동 포함) | "cargo"(전용 화물기, 여객 없음)
    cargoFareMultiplier = 1,
    // 화물 노선일 때 화물 운임 배수(여객 fareMultiplier와 같은 개념)
    overheadRatio = 0.73
    // 직접운항비에 포함되지 않은 인건비·판매비·본사비·IT·보험 등을 포함하는 매출연동 총간접비율.
  }) {
    if (routeType === "cargo") {
      return simulateCargoOnlyRoute({
        airportA,
        airportB,
        distanceKm: distanceKm2,
        aircraft,
        flightsPerWeekOneWay,
        year,
        gameState: gameState2,
        competitorCount,
        costMultiplier,
        cargoFareMultiplier,
        demandMultiplier,
        overheadRatio
      });
    }
    const svcBonus = (serviceQualityBonus(mealTierId, cabinMix, cateringCompanyA, inHouseCateringA) + serviceQualityBonus(mealTierId, cabinMix, cateringCompanyB, inHouseCateringB)) / 2;
    const blendedFareMultiplier = blendFareMultiplier(fareMultiplier, cabinMix);
    const baseDemand = estimateWeeklyDemandBreakdown(
      airportA,
      airportB,
      distanceKm2,
      year,
      tourismLevelA,
      tourismLevelB,
      gameState2,
      { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }
    );
    const fareSensitivity = { business: 0.25, leisure: 0.82, vfr_essential: 0.58 };
    const fareResponse = (purpose) => Math.max(0.42, Math.min(1.35, Math.exp(-fareSensitivity[purpose] * (blendedFareMultiplier - 1))));
    const localDemandByType = {};
    for (const purpose of ["business", "leisure", "vfr_essential"]) {
      localDemandByType[purpose] = (baseDemand.byType[purpose] ?? 0) * Math.max(0, localOdMultiplier) * demandMultiplier * fareResponse(purpose);
    }
    const weeklyDemand = Object.values(localDemandByType).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const normalizeExtra = (value) => {
      if (value && typeof value === "object") return {
        business: Math.max(0, value.business ?? 0),
        leisure: Math.max(0, value.leisure ?? 0),
        vfr_essential: Math.max(0, value.vfr_essential ?? 0)
      };
      const numeric = Math.max(0, Number(value) || 0);
      const shares = baseDemand.shares ?? { business: 0.33, leisure: 0.34, vfr_essential: 0.33 };
      return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(shares).map(([k, v]) => [k, numeric * v]));
    };
    const selfExtraByType = normalizeExtra(selfConnectingDemand);
    const allianceExtraByType = normalizeExtra(allianceConnectingDemand);
    const legacyExtraByType = normalizeExtra(extraDemand);
    for (const purpose of ["business", "leisure", "vfr_essential"]) {
      selfExtraByType[purpose] += legacyExtraByType[purpose];
    }
    const weeklySeatCapacity = aircraft.seats * flightsPerWeekOneWay * 2;
    const marketShareCap = 1 / (1 + competitorCount);
    const demandByType = {};
    const localReachableByType = {};
    for (const purpose of ["business", "leisure", "vfr_essential"]) {
      localReachableByType[purpose] = localDemandByType[purpose] * marketShareCap;
      demandByType[purpose] = localReachableByType[purpose] + selfExtraByType[purpose] + allianceExtraByType[purpose];
    }
    const reachableDemand = Object.values(demandByType).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const routePremiumIdx = routePremiumDemandIndex(airportA, airportB);
    const cabinBreakdown = {};
    let idealTotalPax = 0;
    for (const cls of ["economy", "business", "first"]) {
      const share = cabinMix[cls] ?? 0;
      const seatsInClass = aircraft.seats * share * flightsPerWeekOneWay * 2;
      const classMultiplier = classFareMultiplier(fareMultiplier, cls);
      const classLoadFactor = seatsInClass > 0 ? computeLoadFactor({
        fareMultiplier: classMultiplier,
        competitorCount,
        serviceBonus: svcBonus,
        demandBias: classDemandBias(cls, routePremiumIdx)
      }) : 0;
      const idealPax = seatsInClass * classLoadFactor;
      const baseRatio = CABIN_CLASSES[cls]?.fareMultiplier ?? 1;
      const farePerPaxClass = distanceKm2 * baseFarePerKm(distanceKm2) * baseRatio * classMultiplier;
      cabinBreakdown[cls] = { seats: seatsInClass, idealPax, loadFactor: classLoadFactor, farePerPax: farePerPaxClass };
      idealTotalPax += idealPax;
    }
    const demandScale = idealTotalPax > 0 ? Math.min(1, reachableDemand / idealTotalPax) : 1;
    let paxCarried = 0;
    let weeklyRevenue = 0;
    for (const cls of ["economy", "business", "first"]) {
      const b = cabinBreakdown[cls];
      b.paxCarried = b.idealPax * demandScale;
      b.loadFactor = b.seats > 0 ? b.paxCarried / b.seats : 0;
      b.revenue = b.paxCarried * b.farePerPax;
      paxCarried += b.paxCarried;
      weeklyRevenue += b.revenue;
    }
    const farePerPax = paxCarried > 0 ? weeklyRevenue / paxCarried : 0;
    const loadFactor = weeklySeatCapacity > 0 ? paxCarried / weeklySeatCapacity : 0;
    const passengerAllocationScale = reachableDemand > 0 ? Math.min(1, paxCarried / reachableDemand) : 0;
    const passengerTypeBreakdown = {};
    for (const purpose of ["business", "leisure", "vfr_essential"]) {
      passengerTypeBreakdown[purpose] = (demandByType[purpose] ?? 0) * passengerAllocationScale;
    }
    const localPaxCarried = Object.values(localReachableByType).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) * passengerAllocationScale;
    const selfConnectingPaxCarried = Object.values(selfExtraByType).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) * passengerAllocationScale;
    const allianceConnectingPaxCarried = Object.values(allianceExtraByType).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) * passengerAllocationScale;
    const flightHours = estimateFlightHours(aircraft, distanceKm2);
    const weeklyFlightHours = flightHours * flightsPerWeekOneWay * 2;
    const eraCostMult = costEraMultiplier(year);
    const weeklyFuelCrewMaint = weeklyFlightHours * aircraft.opcost_usd_hr * costMultiplier * eraCostMult;
    const weeklyLandingFees = (landingFeeUsd(airportA, year, gameState2, aircraft) + landingFeeUsd(airportB, year, gameState2, aircraft)) * flightsPerWeekOneWay * eraCostMult;
    const mealCostA = mealCostPerPax(mealTierId, cabinMix, inHouseCateringA, cateringCompanyA);
    const mealCostB = mealCostPerPax(mealTierId, cabinMix, inHouseCateringB, cateringCompanyB);
    const avgMealCostPerPax = (mealCostA + mealCostB) / 2;
    const weeklyMealCost = paxCarried * avgMealCostPerPax;
    const cargo = simulateCargoOperation({ airportA, airportB, distanceKm: distanceKm2, aircraft, flightsPerWeekOneWay, year, gameState: gameState2, competitorCount, passengerLoadFactor: loadFactor, cargoDemandMultiplier: demandMultiplier });
    const SGA_OVERHEAD_RATIO = overheadRatio;
    const weeklySgaOverhead = weeklyRevenue * SGA_OVERHEAD_RATIO;
    const weeklyOperatingCost = weeklyFuelCrewMaint + weeklyLandingFees + weeklyMealCost + cargo.weeklyCargoHandlingCost + weeklySgaOverhead;
    const weeklyRevenueWithCargo = weeklyRevenue + cargo.weeklyCargoRevenue;
    return {
      distanceKm: distanceKm2,
      weeklyDemand,
      metroWeeklyDemand: baseDemand.metroTotal ?? weeklyDemand,
      demandByType,
      passengerTypeBreakdown,
      localPaxCarried,
      selfConnectingPaxCarried,
      allianceConnectingPaxCarried,
      loadFactor,
      weeklySeatCapacity,
      paxCarried,
      farePerPax,
      cabinBreakdown,
      // { economy: {seats, paxCarried, loadFactor, farePerPax, revenue}, business: {...}, first: {...} }
      mealTierId,
      avgMealCostPerPax,
      weeklyMealCost,
      weeklyFuelCrewMaint,
      weeklyLandingFees,
      weeklyPassengerRevenue: weeklyRevenue,
      // 화물 제외 순수 여객 매출(참고용 — 기존 weeklyRevenue와의 하위호환은 아래 필드가 담당)
      weeklyCargoCapacityKg: cargo.weeklyCargoCapacityKg,
      cargoLoadFactor: cargo.cargoLoadFactor,
      cargoKgCarried: cargo.cargoKgCarried,
      weeklyCargoRevenue: cargo.weeklyCargoRevenue,
      weeklyCargoHandlingCost: cargo.weeklyCargoHandlingCost,
      weeklyRevenue: weeklyRevenueWithCargo,
      weeklyOperatingCost,
      weeklyProfit: weeklyRevenueWithCargo - weeklyOperatingCost,
      routeType: "passenger"
    };
  }

  // src/core/capabilities.js
  var capabilities_exports = {};
  __export(capabilities_exports, {
    CAPABILITY_META: () => CAPABILITY_META,
    CAPABILITY_TYPES: () => CAPABILITY_TYPES,
    INVESTMENT_PLANS: () => INVESTMENT_PLANS,
    advanceCapabilities: () => advanceCapabilities,
    estimateQuarterlyInvestment: () => estimateQuarterlyInvestment,
    getCapabilityPlan: () => getCapabilityPlan,
    getCapabilityScore: () => getCapabilityScore,
    industryAverageScore: () => industryAverageScore,
    maintenanceCapabilityCostMultiplier: () => maintenanceCapabilityCostMultiplier,
    maintenanceCapabilityRiskMultiplier: () => maintenanceCapabilityRiskMultiplier,
    marketingDemandMultiplier: () => marketingDemandMultiplier,
    predictNextScore: () => predictNextScore,
    serviceFareToleranceMultiplier: () => serviceFareToleranceMultiplier,
    setCapabilityPlan: () => setCapabilityPlan,
    welfareLaborDisputeRiskMultiplier: () => welfareLaborDisputeRiskMultiplier
  });
  var CAPABILITY_TYPES = ["maintenance", "marketing", "service", "welfare", "it"];
  var CAPABILITY_META = {
    maintenance: { label: "\uC815\uBE44", icon: "\u{1F527}", unit: "\uC815\uBE44\uB825", effectLabel: "\uC0AC\uACE0 \uC704\uD5D8" },
    marketing: { label: "\uAD11\uACE0\xB7\uD64D\uBCF4", icon: "\u{1F4E2}", unit: "\uD64D\uBCF4\uB825", effectLabel: "\uC804\uC0AC \uC218\uC694" },
    service: { label: "\uC11C\uBE44\uC2A4", icon: "\u{1F6CE}\uFE0F", unit: "\uC11C\uBE44\uC2A4\uB825", effectLabel: "\uC6B4\uC784 \uD5C8\uC6A9\uCE58" },
    // 직원복지(사용자 제안 — "노동쟁의를 직원복지라는 투자탭에 추가할 수 있지 않을까").
    // 기존에 events.js에 "노동쟁의"(strike) 이벤트가 이미 있었는데, 사고(accident)와
    // 달리 정비력 같은 어떤 완화 수단도 없이 항상 고정 4% 확률로만 발동하고 있었음 —
    // 이 역량을 그 완화 수단으로 연결함.
    welfare: { label: "\uC9C1\uC6D0\uBCF5\uC9C0", icon: "\u{1F91D}", unit: "\uBCF5\uC9C0\uB825", effectLabel: "\uB178\uB3D9\uC7C1\uC758 \uC704\uD5D8" },
    it: { label: "IT\u00B7\uC608\uC57D", icon: "\u{1F4BB}", unit: "IT\uC5ED\uB7C9", effectLabel: "\uC9C1\uC811\uD310\uB9E4\u00B7\uC218\uC775\uAD00\uB9AC" }
  };
  var INVESTMENT_PLANS = {
    max: { label: "\uCD5C\uB300\uD22C\uC790", costRatio: 25e-4, targetScore: 100, speed: 9 },
    normal: { label: "\uD1B5\uC0C1\uD22C\uC790", costRatio: 1e-3, targetScore: 72, speed: 4 },
    maintain: { label: "\uD604\uC0C1\uC720\uC9C0", costRatio: 35e-5, targetScore: null, speed: 0 },
    // 현재 점수를 목표로 삼음(유지비만 나감)
    reduce: { label: "\uD22C\uC790\uCD95\uC18C", costRatio: 1e-4, targetScore: 25, speed: 3 },
    stop: { label: "\uD22C\uC790\uC911\uB2E8", costRatio: 0, targetScore: 0, speed: 6 }
  };
  var MIN_TOTAL_CAPABILITY_INVESTMENT_USD = 5e4;
  var MAX_TOTAL_CAPABILITY_INVESTMENT_USD = 2e5;
  function ensureCapabilities(airline) {
    var _a;
    airline.capabilities ?? (airline.capabilities = {});
    for (const type of CAPABILITY_TYPES) {
      (_a = airline.capabilities)[type] ?? (_a[type] = { score: 50, plan: "normal" });
    }
    return airline.capabilities;
  }
  function getCapabilityScore(airline, type) {
    return ensureCapabilities(airline)[type].score;
  }
  function getCapabilityPlan(airline, type) {
    return ensureCapabilities(airline)[type].plan;
  }
  function setCapabilityPlan(airline, type, plan) {
    if (!CAPABILITY_TYPES.includes(type)) throw new Error(`\uC54C \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uC5ED\uB7C9 \uC885\uB958: ${type}`);
    if (!INVESTMENT_PLANS[plan]) throw new Error(`\uC54C \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uD22C\uC790 \uACC4\uD68D: ${plan}`);
    ensureCapabilities(airline)[type].plan = plan;
  }
  function minimumCapabilityInvestmentTotal(airline) {
    const operatingScale = (airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) + (airline.routes?.length ?? 0);
    return Math.min(MAX_TOTAL_CAPABILITY_INVESTMENT_USD, MIN_TOTAL_CAPABILITY_INVESTMENT_USD + operatingScale * 5e3);
  }
  function estimateQuarterlyInvestment(airline, type, fleetValueUsd) {
    const plan = INVESTMENT_PLANS[getCapabilityPlan(airline, type)];
    if (plan.costRatio <= 0) return 0;
    const minimumPerCapability = minimumCapabilityInvestmentTotal(airline) / CAPABILITY_TYPES.length;
    return Math.max(minimumPerCapability, fleetValueUsd * plan.costRatio);
  }
  function predictNextScore(airline, type) {
    const cap = ensureCapabilities(airline)[type];
    const plan = INVESTMENT_PLANS[cap.plan];
    const target = plan.targetScore ?? cap.score;
    const delta = Math.sign(target - cap.score) * Math.min(plan.speed, Math.abs(target - cap.score));
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, cap.score + delta));
  }
  function advanceCapabilities(airline, fleetValueUsd) {
    const caps = ensureCapabilities(airline);
    const spent = {};
    for (const type of CAPABILITY_TYPES) {
      const cap = caps[type];
      const cost = estimateQuarterlyInvestment(airline, type, fleetValueUsd);
      if (cost > 0 && airline.cash < cost) {
        spent[type] = 0;
        continue;
      }
      airline.cash -= cost;
      spent[type] = cost;
      const plan = INVESTMENT_PLANS[cap.plan];
      const target = plan.targetScore ?? cap.score;
      const delta = Math.sign(target - cap.score) * Math.min(plan.speed, Math.abs(target - cap.score));
      cap.score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, cap.score + delta));
    }
    return spent;
  }
  function industryAverageScore(airlines, type) {
    if (!airlines || airlines.length === 0) return 50;
    const total = airlines.reduce((sum, a) => sum + getCapabilityScore(a, type), 0);
    return total / airlines.length;
  }
  function maintenanceCapabilityRiskMultiplier(airline) {
    const score = getCapabilityScore(airline, "maintenance");
    return 1.3 - score / 100 * 0.9;
  }
  function maintenanceCapabilityCostMultiplier(airline) {
    const score = getCapabilityScore(airline, "maintenance");
    // 정비투자는 사고위험뿐 아니라 고장·비계획 정비비도 줄인다.
    return 1.08 - score / 100 * 0.16;
  }
  function marketingDemandMultiplier(airline) {
    const score = getCapabilityScore(airline, "marketing");
    const capabilityEffect = 0.95 + score / 100 * 0.17;
    const essentialServiceSupport = airline.segment === "commuter" ? 1.15 : 1;
    return capabilityEffect * essentialServiceSupport;
  }
  function serviceFareToleranceMultiplier(airline) {
    const score = getCapabilityScore(airline, "service");
    return 0.97 + score / 100 * 0.11;
  }
  function welfareLaborDisputeRiskMultiplier(airline) {
    const score = getCapabilityScore(airline, "welfare");
    return 1.3 - score / 100 * 1;
  }

  // src/core/alliances.js
  var ALLIANCES = {
    "Star Alliance": { id: "Star Alliance", label: "\uC2A4\uD0C0\uC5BC\uB77C\uC774\uC5B8\uC2A4", founded: 1997, color: "#0056A3" },
    SkyTeam: { id: "SkyTeam", label: "\uC2A4\uCE74\uC774\uD300", founded: 2e3, color: "#E4022C" },
    oneworld: { id: "oneworld", label: "\uC6D0\uC6D4\uB4DC", founded: 1999, color: "#1A1A1A" }
  };
  var ALLIANCE_IDS = Object.keys(ALLIANCES);
  var ALLIANCE_MIN_ROUTES = 5;
  var ALLIANCE_MIN_FLEET = 5;
  var ALLIANCE_MIN_CAPABILITY_SCORE = 55;
  function averageCapabilityScore(airline) {
    const types = CAPABILITY_TYPES;
    return types.reduce((sum, t) => sum + getCapabilityScore(airline, t), 0) / types.length;
  }
  function allianceJoinStatus(airline, allianceId, gameState2) {
    const alliance = ALLIANCES[allianceId];
    const checks = {
      exists: !!alliance,
      eraOk: alliance ? gameState2.year >= alliance.founded : false,
      notAlreadyMember: !airline.alliance,
      routesOk: airline.routes.length >= ALLIANCE_MIN_ROUTES,
      fleetOk: airline.fleet.length >= ALLIANCE_MIN_FLEET,
      capabilityOk: averageCapabilityScore(airline) >= ALLIANCE_MIN_CAPABILITY_SCORE,
      financeOk: airline.cash >= 0
    };
    const ok = Object.values(checks).every(Boolean);
    return {
      ok,
      checks,
      progress: {
        routes: `${airline.routes.length}/${ALLIANCE_MIN_ROUTES}`,
        fleet: `${airline.fleet.length}/${ALLIANCE_MIN_FLEET}`,
        capability: `${averageCapabilityScore(airline).toFixed(0)}/${ALLIANCE_MIN_CAPABILITY_SCORE}`
      }
    };
  }
  function joinAlliance(airline, allianceId, gameState2) {
    const status = allianceJoinStatus(airline, allianceId, gameState2);
    if (!status.ok) {
      const reasons = [];
      if (!status.checks.exists) reasons.push("\uC874\uC7AC\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uB294 \uC5BC\uB77C\uC774\uC5B8\uC2A4\uC785\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
      if (status.checks.exists && !status.checks.eraOk) reasons.push(`${ALLIANCES[allianceId].label}\uC740(\uB294) ${ALLIANCES[allianceId].founded}\uB144\uC5D0 \uCC3D\uC124\uB418\uC5B4 \uC544\uC9C1 \uC874\uC7AC\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
      if (!status.checks.notAlreadyMember) reasons.push("\uC774\uBBF8 \uB2E4\uB978 \uC5BC\uB77C\uC774\uC5B8\uC2A4\uC5D0 \uAC00\uC785\uB418\uC5B4 \uC788\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4(\uBA3C\uC800 \uD0C8\uD1F4 \uD544\uC694).");
      if (!status.checks.routesOk) reasons.push(`\uB178\uC120 ${ALLIANCE_MIN_ROUTES}\uAC1C \uC774\uC0C1 \uD544\uC694(\uD604\uC7AC ${airline.routes.length}\uAC1C).`);
      if (!status.checks.fleetOk) reasons.push(`\uBCF4\uC720 \uAE30\uC7AC ${ALLIANCE_MIN_FLEET}\uB300 \uC774\uC0C1 \uD544\uC694(\uD604\uC7AC ${airline.fleet.length}\uB300).`);
      if (!status.checks.capabilityOk) reasons.push(`\uAE30\uC5C5 \uC5ED\uB7C9 \uD3C9\uADE0 ${ALLIANCE_MIN_CAPABILITY_SCORE}\uC810 \uC774\uC0C1 \uD544\uC694(\uD604\uC7AC ${averageCapabilityScore(airline).toFixed(0)}\uC810).`);
      if (!status.checks.financeOk) reasons.push("\uD604\uAE08 \uBCF4\uC720\uACE0\uAC00 \uB9C8\uC774\uB108\uC2A4\uB77C \uC7AC\uBB34 \uC2EC\uC0AC\uB97C \uD1B5\uACFC\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
      throw new Error(reasons.join(" "));
    }
    airline.alliance = allianceId;
    airline.allianceJoinedYear = gameState2.year;
  }
  function leaveAlliance(airline) {
    if (!airline.alliance) throw new Error("\uAC00\uC785\uB41C \uC5BC\uB77C\uC774\uC5B8\uC2A4\uAC00 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    airline.alliance = null;
    airline.allianceJoinedYear = null;
  }
  function alliancePartners(airline, gameState2) {
    if (!airline.alliance) return [];
    return gameState2.airlines.filter((a) => a.id !== airline.id && a.alliance === airline.alliance);
  }

    (_a = airline.subsidiaries).facilities ?? (_a.facilities = []);
    return airline.subsidiaries;
  }

      revenuePerQuarter: (size, level, year) => size * level * 5e3 * costEraMultiplier(year),
      regional: { countries: ["JP", "IS", "TR"] }
    },
    safari_lodge: {
      label: "\u{1F981} \uC0AC\uD30C\uB9AC \uB86F\uC9C0",
      cost: (airport) => 3e6 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 18e5,
      tourismBoost: 3,
      revenuePerQuarter: (size, level, year) => size * level * 6500 * costEraMultiplier(year),
      regional: { countries: ["KE", "TZ", "ZA"] }
    },
    winery_tour: {
      label: "\u{1F377} \uC640\uC774\uB108\uB9AC \uD22C\uC5B4",
      cost: (airport) => 22e5 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 13e5,
      tourismBoost: 2,
      revenuePerQuarter: (size, level, year) => size * level * 5200 * costEraMultiplier(year),
      regional: { countries: ["FR", "IT", "CL", "AR"] }
    },
    trekking_camp: {
      label: "\u{1F3D4}\uFE0F \uD2B8\uB808\uD0B9 \uBCA0\uC774\uC2A4\uCEA0\uD504",
      cost: (airport) => 1e6 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 5e5,
      tourismBoost: 2,
      revenuePerQuarter: (size, level, year) => size * level * 3200 * costEraMultiplier(year),
      regional: { countries: ["NP", "BT"] }
    }
  };
  function isFacilityAvailableAt(type, airport) {
    const def = FACILITY_TYPES[type];
    if (!def) return false;
    if (!def.regional) return true;
    const { countries, airports } = def.regional;
    if (countries?.includes(airport.country)) return true;
    if (airports?.includes(airport.iata)) return true;
    return false;
  }
  function availableFacilityTypesAt(airport) {
    return Object.keys(FACILITY_TYPES).filter((type) => isFacilityAvailableAt(type, airport));
  }
  function facilityCost(type, airport) {
    const def = FACILITY_TYPES[type];
    if (!def) throw new Error(`\uC54C \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uC2DC\uC124 \uC885\uB958: ${type}`);
    return def.cost(airport);
  }
  function buildFacility(airline, airport, gameState2, type, { year }) {
    const def = FACILITY_TYPES[type];
    if (!def) throw new Error(`\uC54C \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uC2DC\uC124 \uC885\uB958: ${type}`);
    if (!isFacilityAvailableAt(type, airport)) {
      throw new Error(`${def.label}\uC740(\uB294) ${airport.city ?? airport.iata}\uC5D0\uC11C \uC9C0\uC744 \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uC9C0\uC5ED \uD2B9\uC0B0 \uBD80\uB300\uC0AC\uC5C5\uC785\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
    }
    const cost = def.cost(airport);
    if (airline.cash < cost) {
      throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: ${airport.city ?? airport.iata} ${def.label} \uAC74\uC124\uC5D0 $${cost.toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694`);
    }
    airline.cash -= cost;
    const subs = ensureSubsidiaries(airline);
    subs.facilities.push({ type, airportIata: airport.iata, builtYear: year, level: 1 });
    if (def.tourismBoost > 0) {
      gameState2.cityTourismLevel ?? (gameState2.cityTourismLevel = {});
      const current = gameState2.cityTourismLevel[airport.iata] ?? 0;
      gameState2.cityTourismLevel[airport.iata] = Math.min(MAX_TOURISM_LEVEL, current + def.tourismBoost);
    }
    return subs.facilities[subs.facilities.length - 1];
  }
  function hasFacility(airline, airportIata, type) {
    return airline.subsidiaries?.facilities?.some((f) => f.airportIata === airportIata && f.type === type) ?? false;
  }
  function facilityRevenueBreakdown(airline, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }) {
    const subs = ensureSubsidiaries(airline);
    const entries = [];
    for (const f of subs.facilities) {
      const airport = airportsByIata2[f.airportIata];
      if (!airport) continue;
      const def = FACILITY_TYPES[f.type];
      const tourismLevel = gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[airport.iata] ?? 0;
      const size = marketSize(airport, tourismLevel, gameState2.year, gameState2);
      entries.push({
        kind: "facility",
        type: f.type,
        label: def?.label ?? f.type,
        airportIata: f.airportIata,
        city: airport.city ?? airport.iata,
        quarterlyRevenue: def.revenuePerQuarter(size, f.level, gameState2.year),
        quarterlyCost: 0
      });
    }
    for (const catering of subs.catering) {
      const airport = airportsByIata2[catering.airportIata];
      if (!airport) continue;
      entries.push({
        kind: "catering",
        type: "self_built_catering",
        label: "\uC790\uCCB4 \uCF00\uC774\uD130\uB9C1 \uC2DC\uC124(\uC678\uBD80\uD310\uB9E4)",
        airportIata: catering.airportIata,
        city: airport.city ?? airport.iata,
        quarterlyRevenue: cateringExternalSalesRevenue(airline, catering, gameState2, airport),
        quarterlyCost: cateringQuarterlyMaintenance(airport)
      });
    }
    return entries;
  }
  function computeAncillaryStatement(airline, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }) {
    const breakdown = facilityRevenueBreakdown(airline, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 });
    const revenue = breakdown.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.quarterlyRevenue, 0);
    const cost = breakdown.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.quarterlyCost, 0);
    return { revenue, cost, profit: revenue - cost };
  }
  var FACILITY_EARNINGS_MULTIPLE = 4;
  var FACILITY_SALVAGE_FLOOR_RATIO = 0.3;
  function facilitySaleValue(airline, target, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }) {
    const airport = airportsByIata2[target.airportIata];
    if (!airport) return 0;
    const breakdown = facilityRevenueBreakdown(airline, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 });
    const entry = breakdown.find(
      (b) => b.kind === target.kind && b.airportIata === target.airportIata && (target.kind !== "facility" || b.type === target.type)
    );
    const quarterlyProfit = entry ? entry.quarterlyRevenue - entry.quarterlyCost : 0;
    const annualProfit = quarterlyProfit * 4;
    const earningsBasedValue = Math.max(0, annualProfit * FACILITY_EARNINGS_MULTIPLE);
    const originalCost = target.kind === "facility" ? facilityCost(target.type, airport) : cateringCost(airport);
    const salvageFloor = originalCost * FACILITY_SALVAGE_FLOOR_RATIO;
    return Math.max(salvageFloor, earningsBasedValue);
  }
  function sellFacility(airline, target, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }) {
    const subs = ensureSubsidiaries(airline);
    const saleValue = facilitySaleValue(airline, target, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 });
    if (target.kind === "facility") {
      const idx = subs.facilities.findIndex((f) => f.airportIata === target.airportIata && f.type === target.type);
      if (idx === -1) throw new Error("\uBCF4\uC720\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uC740 \uC2DC\uC124\uC785\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
      subs.facilities.splice(idx, 1);
    } else if (target.kind === "catering") {
      const idx = subs.catering.findIndex((c) => c.airportIata === target.airportIata);
      if (idx === -1) throw new Error("\uBCF4\uC720\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uC740 \uCF00\uC774\uD130\uB9C1 \uC2DC\uC124\uC785\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
      subs.catering.splice(idx, 1);
    } else {
      throw new Error(`\uC54C \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uB9E4\uAC01 \uB300\uC0C1: ${target.kind}`);
    }
    airline.cash += saleValue;
    return saleValue;
  }
  function cateringCost(airport) {
    return 12e5 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 8e5;
  }
  function cateringQuarterlyMaintenance(airport) {
    return 5e4 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 2e4;
  }
  function buildCatering(airline, airport, { year }) {
    const cost = cateringCost(airport);
    if (airline.cash < cost) {
      throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: ${airport.city ?? airport.iata} \uCF00\uC774\uD130\uB9C1 \uC2DC\uC124 \uAC74\uC124\uC5D0 $${cost.toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694`);
    }
    airline.cash -= cost;
    const subs = ensureSubsidiaries(airline);
    subs.catering.push({ airportIata: airport.iata, builtYear: year });
  }
  function hasCateringAt(airline, airportIata) {
    return airline.subsidiaries?.catering?.some((c) => c.airportIata === airportIata) ?? false;
  }
  function countOtherAirlinesAt(gameState2, selfAirlineId, iata) {
    let count = 0;
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines ?? []) {
      if (airline.id === selfAirlineId) continue;
      if (airline.routes.some((r) => r.a === iata || r.b === iata)) count++;
    }
    return count;
  }
  function cateringExternalSalesRevenue(airline, catering, gameState2, airport) {
    const others = countOtherAirlinesAt(gameState2, airline.id, catering.airportIata);
    const tierFactor = airport ? airport.hub_tier ?? 3 : 3;
    return others * tierFactor * 6e3 * costEraMultiplier(gameState2.year);
  }
  var CONTRACT_SIGNING_FEE_BASE = 2e5;
  function cateringContractSigningFee(cateringCompany) {
    return CONTRACT_SIGNING_FEE_BASE * cateringCompany.price_multiplier;
  }
  function contractCatering(airline, airport, cateringCompany, { year }) {
    const fee = cateringContractSigningFee(cateringCompany);
    if (airline.cash < fee) {
      throw new Error(`\uACC4\uC57D\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: ${cateringCompany.name}\uACFC(\uC640) ${airport.iata} \uACC4\uC57D\uC5D0 $${fee.toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694`);
    }
    airline.cash -= fee;
    const subs = ensureSubsidiaries(airline);
    subs.cateringContracts ?? (subs.cateringContracts = {});
    subs.cateringContracts[airport.iata] = { companyId: cateringCompany.id, signedYear: year };
  }
  function cancelCateringContract(airline, airportIata) {
    if (airline.subsidiaries?.cateringContracts) {
      delete airline.subsidiaries.cateringContracts[airportIata];
    }
  }
  function getCateringCompanyAt(airline, airportIata, cateringById2) {
    const contract = airline.subsidiaries?.cateringContracts?.[airportIata];
    if (!contract) return null;
    return cateringById2[contract.companyId] ?? null;
  }

  // src/core/relations.js
  function interpolate2(curve, year) {
    if (year <= curve[0][0]) return curve[0][1];
    const last = curve[curve.length - 1];
    if (year >= last[0]) return last[1];
    for (let i = 0; i < curve.length - 1; i++) {
      const [y0, v0] = curve[i];
      const [y1, v1] = curve[i + 1];
      if (year >= y0 && year <= y1) {
        const t = (year - y0) / (y1 - y0);
        return v0 + t * (v1 - v0);
      }
    }
    return last[1];
  }

  function blocOf(country, year, gameState2) {
    const alt = gameState2?.altHistory;
    if (alt?.kmt_wins_civil_war && country === "CN" && year >= 1949) return "west";
    if (alt?.bay_of_pigs_succeeds && country === "CU" && year >= 1961) return "west";
    if (alt?.iran_shah_retains_power && country === "IR" && year >= 1979) return "west";
    if (alt?.korea_unifies_1950_south && country === "KP" && year >= 1950) return "west";
    if (country === "CN") return "china";
    if (country === "KP") return "dprk";
    if (country === "CU") return "cuba";
    if (SOVIET_CORE.has(country)) return "russia";
    if (NATO_EASTWARD_1999.has(country)) return year >= 1999 ? "west" : "east_bloc_former";
    if (NATO_EASTWARD_2004.has(country)) return year >= 2004 ? "west" : "east_bloc_former";
    if (POST_SOVIET_OTHER.has(country)) return year >= 1992 ? "nonaligned" : "east_bloc_former";
    if (WEST_CORE.has(country)) return "west";
    return "nonaligned";
  }

  function getBlocPairRelation(blocA, blocB, year, gameState2) {
    if (blocA === blocB) return 78;
    if (blocA === "nonaligned" || blocB === "nonaligned") return 75;
    const involvesRussiaBloc = blocA === "russia" || blocB === "russia" || blocA === "east_bloc_former" || blocB === "east_bloc_former";
    let effectiveYear = year;
    if (gameState2?.altHistory?.soviet_coup_succeeds && involvesRussiaBloc) {
      effectiveYear = Math.min(effectiveYear, 1984);
    }
    let russia1993Cap = null;
    if (gameState2?.altHistory?.russia_1993_hardliners_win && involvesRussiaBloc) {
      russia1993Cap = 50;
    }
    const key1 = `${blocA}-${blocB}`;
    const key2 = `${blocB}-${blocA}`;
    const trajectory = BLOC_RELATION_TRAJECTORY[key1] ?? BLOC_RELATION_TRAJECTORY[key2];
    if (!trajectory) return 75;
    const value = interpolate2(trajectory, effectiveYear);
    return russia1993Cap != null ? Math.min(value, russia1993Cap) : value;
  }

  function apartheidIsolationPenalty(countryA, countryB, year) {
    if (year < 1960 || year >= 1994) return 0;
    const other = countryA === "ZA" ? countryB : countryB === "ZA" ? countryA : null;
    if (!other) return 0;
    return -30;
  }
  function pairKey(a, b) {
    return [a, b].sort().join("-");
  }
  var MANUFACTURER_COUNTRY_ALIAS = { SU: "RU" };
  function normalizeManufacturerCountry(country) {
    return MANUFACTURER_COUNTRY_ALIAS[country] ?? country;
  }
  var AIRCRAFT_PURCHASE_BLOCK_THRESHOLD = 15;
  function canPurchaseAircraft(airlineCountry, manufacturerCountry, year, gameState2) {
    if (!airlineCountry || !manufacturerCountry) return true;
    const mfg = normalizeManufacturerCountry(manufacturerCountry);
    if (mfg === airlineCountry) return true;
    return getRelationScore(airlineCountry, mfg, year, gameState2) >= AIRCRAFT_PURCHASE_BLOCK_THRESHOLD;
  }
  function getRelationScore(countryA, countryB, year, gameState2) {
    if (!countryA || !countryB || countryA === countryB) return 100;
    const key = pairKey(countryA, countryB);
    const override = gameState2?.relationOverrides?.[key];
    if (override != null) return override;
    let score;
    const alt = gameState2?.altHistory;
    if (alt?.kmt_wins_civil_war && key === "CN-TW") {
      score = 95;
    } else if (alt?.taiwan_falls_1950 && key === "CN-TW") {
      score = 95;
    } else if (alt?.south_vietnam_wins && key === "US-VN") {
      score = year >= 1975 ? 78 : interpolate2(BILATERAL_OVERRIDES["US-VN"], year);
    } else if (alt?.korea_unifies_1950_south && key === "KP-KR") {
      score = year >= 1950 ? 100 : interpolate2(BILATERAL_OVERRIDES["KP-KR"], year);
    } else if (alt?.iran_shah_retains_power && key === "IR-US") {
      score = year >= 1979 ? 65 : interpolate2(BILATERAL_OVERRIDES["IR-US"], year);
    } else if (alt?.six_day_war_arabs_win && (key === "EG-IL" || key === "IL-JO")) {
      score = year >= 1967 ? 12 : interpolate2(BILATERAL_OVERRIDES[key], year);
    } else {
      const bilateral = BILATERAL_OVERRIDES[key];
      if (bilateral) {
        score = interpolate2(bilateral, year);
      } else {
        const blocA = blocOf(countryA, year, gameState2);
        const blocB = blocOf(countryB, year, gameState2);
        score = getBlocPairRelation(blocA, blocB, year, gameState2);
        score += apartheidIsolationPenalty(countryA, countryB, year);
      }
    }
    const tempAdjust = gameState2?.relationTempAdjust?.[key];
    if (tempAdjust) score += tempAdjust;
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(score)));
  }
  function setRelationOverride(gameState2, countryA, countryB, score) {
    gameState2.relationOverrides ?? (gameState2.relationOverrides = {});
    gameState2.relationOverrides[pairKey(countryA, countryB)] = score;
  }
  function adjustRelationTemporarily(gameState2, countryA, countryB, delta, quarters) {
    gameState2.relationTempAdjust ?? (gameState2.relationTempAdjust = {});
    gameState2.relationTempAdjustRemaining ?? (gameState2.relationTempAdjustRemaining = {});
    const key = pairKey(countryA, countryB);
    gameState2.relationTempAdjust[key] = (gameState2.relationTempAdjust[key] ?? 0) + delta;
    gameState2.relationTempAdjustRemaining[key] = Math.max(
      gameState2.relationTempAdjustRemaining[key] ?? 0,
      quarters
    );
  }
  function decayRelationAdjustments(gameState2) {
    const remaining = gameState2.relationTempAdjustRemaining;
    if (!remaining) return;
    for (const key of Object.keys(remaining)) {
      remaining[key] -= 1;
      if (remaining[key] <= 0) {
        delete remaining[key];
        delete gameState2.relationTempAdjust[key];
      }
    }
  }
