  function pickWeightedHost(year, gameState2, recentHosts) {
    const weights = HOST_CANDIDATE_COUNTRIES.map((c) => {
      const econ = Math.max(0.05, countryEconomyMultiplier(c, year, gameState2));
      const recencyPenalty = recentHosts.includes(c) ? 0.1 : 1;
      return { country: c, weight: Math.pow(econ, 1.5) * recencyPenalty };
    });
    const total = weights.reduce((s, w) => s + w.weight, 0);
    let roll = randomUnitV253() * total;
    for (const w of weights) {
      roll -= w.weight;
      if (roll <= 0) return w.country;
    }
    return weights[weights.length - 1].country;
  }
  function representativeAirport(country, airports) {
    const inCountry = airports.filter((a) => a.country === country);
    if (inCountry.length === 0) return null;
    inCountry.sort((a, b) => (b.hub_tier ?? 0) - (a.hub_tier ?? 0));
    return inCountry[0];
  }
  function ensureMegaEventSchedule(gameState2, { airports }, currentYear = 2051) {
    var _a, _b;
    gameState2.megaEventHosts ?? (gameState2.megaEventHosts = {});
    const horizon = Math.max(2051, currentYear + 8);
    for (const set of MEGA_EVENT_SETS) {
      (_a = gameState2.megaEventHosts)[_b = set.key] ?? (_a[_b] = [...set.historical]);
      const schedule = gameState2.megaEventHosts[set.key];
      let lastKnownYear = schedule[schedule.length - 1][0];
      if (lastKnownYear >= horizon) continue;
      const recentHosts = schedule.slice(-3).map((h) => h[3]);
      for (let year = lastKnownYear + set.intervalYears; year <= horizon; year += set.intervalYears) {
        const country = pickWeightedHost(year, gameState2, recentHosts);
        const airport = representativeAirport(country, airports);
        if (!airport) continue;
        schedule.push([year, `[\uAC00\uC0C1] ${airport.city ?? airport.name}`, airport.iata, country]);
        recentHosts.push(country);
        if (recentHosts.length > 3) recentHosts.shift();
      }
    }
  }
  function checkMegaEvents(gameState2, { airports }, pushEvent2) {
    ensureMegaEventSchedule(gameState2, { airports }, gameState2.year);
    gameState2.firedMegaEvents ?? (gameState2.firedMegaEvents = []);
    const fired = new Set(gameState2.firedMegaEvents);
    for (const set of MEGA_EVENT_SETS) {
      const schedule = gameState2.megaEventHosts[set.key] ?? [];
      for (const [year, city, iata, country] of schedule) {
        const id = `${set.key}-${year}`;
        if (year !== gameState2.year || fired.has(id)) continue;
        fired.add(id);
        gameState2.firedMegaEvents.push(id);
        const isVirtual = city.startsWith("[\uAC00\uC0C1]");
        pushEvent2(gameState2, {
          type: "mega_event",
          scope: `airport:${iata}`,
          demandMultiplier: set.boost,
          costMultiplier: 1,
          remainingQuarters: set.quarters,
          label: `\u{1F3DF}\uFE0F ${gameState2.year}\uB144 ${set.label} \uAC1C\uCD5C: ${city}(${iata}) \uAD00\uAD11\uC218\uC694 +${Math.round((set.boost - 1) * 100)}% (${set.quarters}\uBD84\uAE30)${isVirtual ? " \u2014 \uAC00\uC0C1\uC5ED\uC0AC" : ""}`
        });
      }
    }
  }

  function checkHsrNews(gameState2, pushEvent2) {
    gameState2.firedHsrNews ?? (gameState2.firedHsrNews = []);
    const fired = new Set(gameState2.firedHsrNews);
    for (const n of HSR_NEWS) {
      const id = `hsr-${n.country}-${n.year}`;
      if (n.year !== gameState2.year || fired.has(id)) continue;
      fired.add(id);
      gameState2.firedHsrNews.push(id);
      pushEvent2(gameState2, {
        type: "hsr_news",
        scope: "global",
        demandMultiplier: 1,
        costMultiplier: 1,
        remainingQuarters: 1,
        label: n.label
      });
    }
  }

  // src/core/events.js
  function setAltHistoryFlag(gameState2, flag) {
    gameState2.altHistory ?? (gameState2.altHistory = {});
    gameState2.altHistory[flag] = true;
  }
  var REGIONS = [
    "north_america",

    "middle_east_south_asia",
    "africa",
    "east_southeast_asia",
    "oceania"
  ];
  function pick(arr) {
    return arr[Math.floor(randomUnitV253() * arr.length)];
  }
  function pushEvent(gameState2, event) {
    gameState2.activeEvents ?? (gameState2.activeEvents = []);
    gameState2.activeEvents.push(event);
    gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
    gameState2.eventLog.push({ year: gameState2.year, quarter: gameState2.quarter, ...event });
  }
